# 08 Trinity pillars and unresolved obligations

Freed ID and CBR Heart are the priority. Typed selectors, atomic correction records, exact receipt bindings, and retained failures improve inspectability. # 09 Method Flow and nonerasure

This phase retains 1,333 negatives, 1,333 methods, 1,333 failed witnesses, and 1,333 bounded recovery witnesses. The count includes 1,000 registered envelope mutations, 300 initial correction failures, 15 operational events, ten skill adversaries, five shared-runner adversaries, and three package adversaries. These categories are not separate empirical replications.

- `MF6862-OP001`: Initial batched guidance and registry display was truncated. Recovery: Use exact filenames, scalar fields, retained tool results, and bounded complete reads; source bytes remain unchanged. Initial success credit remains zero.

- `MF6862-OP002`: Combined release and index display was truncated. Recovery: Use exact filenames, scalar fields, retained tool results, and bounded complete reads; source bytes remain unchanged. Initial success credit remains zero.

- `MF6862-OP003`: Undated workflow-profile reference did not exist; dated authorized profile found. Recovery: Use exact filenames, scalar fields, retained tool results, and bounded complete reads; source bytes remain unchanged. Initial success credit remains zero.

- `MF6862-OP004`: Sparse materialization completed but its 200-heading output was truncated. Recovery: Use exact filenames, scalar fields, retained tool results, and bounded complete reads; source bytes remain unchanged. Initial success credit remains zero.

- `MF6862-OP005`: Repeated-paragraph baton view had one truncated span; exact missing span reread. Recovery: Use exact filenames, scalar fields, retained tool results, and bounded complete reads; source bytes remain unchanged. Initial success credit remains zero.

- `MF6862-OP006`: One read-only Git inventory outlived its display call; scalar source reads recovered observations. Recovery: Use exact filenames, scalar fields, retained tool results, and bounded complete reads; source bytes remain unchanged. Initial success credit remains zero.

- `MF6862-OP007`: Broad source novelty JSON display was truncated; scope and exact structured input inspected separately. Recovery: Use exact filenames, scalar fields, retained tool results, and bounded complete reads; source bytes remain unchanged. Initial success credit remains zero.

- `MF6862-OP008`: Assumed Elaren toolchain-plan filename was absent; actual package-plan inspected. Recovery: Use exact filenames, scalar fields, retained tool results, and bounded complete reads; source bytes remain unchanged. Initial success credit remains zero.

- `MF6862-OP009`: Assumed next-owner idea field names returned null; actual keys were inspected. Recovery: Use exact filenames, scalar fields, retained tool results, and bounded complete reads; source bytes remain unchanged. Initial success credit remains zero.

- `MF6862-OP010`: Source skill-plan projection exceeded JSON depth and display budget; exact required fields read structurally. Recovery: Use exact filenames, scalar fields, retained tool results, and bounded complete reads; source bytes remain unchanged. Initial success credit remains zero.

- `MF6862-OP011`: Preliminary drive-path regex matched URL scheme suffixes. Recovery: Require a nonalphanumeric left boundary; all exact planning JSON files passed the corrected bounded check. Initial success credit remains zero.

- `MF6862-OP012`: Explicit null permissions inherited the omitted-field default. Recovery: Retain original bytes and reject explicit null; 80 frozen patch cases plus the new null refusal pass. Initial success credit remains zero.

- `MF6862-OP013`: Meta Tool Box promotion policy was checked after global copying rather than before it. Recovery: Ten packages already had local metadata and actual CLI smoke passes; all ten later policy checks are ready and all global byte copies match. Retain the ordering defect. Initial success credit remains zero.

- `MF6862-OP014`: The first global metadata wrapper returned nonzero without preserving its diagnostic; it omitted explicit UTF-8 mode. Recovery: Explicit UTF-8 validation of all ten exact global copies passed. The missing initial diagnostic remains an observability limitation rather than an asserted root cause. Initial success credit remains zero.

- `MF6862-OP015`: The bundled renderer probe could not import fitz. Recovery: Inspect installed modules and use existing pypdfium2 for PDF rendering; install no additional package. Initial success credit remains zero.

Effective evidence-layer totals are 68,005 negatives, 84,500 methods, 38,853 failed witnesses, 66,345 bounded passing witnesses, 612 gaps, and 599 exact gates. Later operational events, if any, belong in a separate overlay and must not rewrite this seal.

# 10 Three exact package additions

- `jmespath 1.1.0`: official wheel `jmespath-1.1.0-py3-none-any.whl`, SHA-256 `a5663118de4908c91729bea0acadca56526eb2698e83de10cd116ae0f4e97c64`. [Exact release](https://pypi.org/project/jmespath/1.1.0/).

- `dpath 2.2.0`: official wheel `dpath-2.2.0-py3-none-any.whl`, SHA-256 `b330a375ded0a0d2ed404440f6c6a715deae5313af40bbb01c8a41d891900576`. [Exact release](https://pypi.org/project/dpath/2.2.0/).

- `dictdiffer 0.10.0`: official wheel `dictdiffer-0.10.0-py3-none-any.whl`, SHA-256 `6ca50f38f7c5ee27d52789eee095ae473d9e02e1aa7612cb3aaf25fcf081dbf6`. [Exact release](https://pypi.org/project/dictdiffer/0.10.0/).

The new isolated D environment contains exactly three distributions. Installation used the frozen wheelhouse offline with required hashes and no runtime dependencies or optional extras. System Python, PATH, the npm prefix, plugin caches, host security, Windows features, and accounts were not changed. Nine package checks passed, including three retained adversaries. The dated OSV query returned zero findings; it does not establish exhaustive security or future safety. Query null dropping remains visible, and mutable helpers work only on synthetic copies. Rollback selects retained prior tooling and preserves the environment and receipts.

---

# 11 Skills runners and successor ideas

- `ghc-family-pointer-codec-resolution` combines `pointer_token_codec` and `pointer_value_resolution` with frozen source criteria and portable helpers.

- `ghc-family-pointer-refusal-projection` combines `pointer_failure_semantics` and `pointer_projection_allowlist` with frozen source criteria and portable helpers.

- `ghc-family-patch-add-remove` combines `patch_add_targets` and `patch_remove_targets` with frozen source criteria and portable helpers.

- `ghc-family-patch-replace-test` combines `patch_replace_targets` and `patch_typed_tests` with frozen source criteria and portable helpers.

- `ghc-family-patch-copy-move` combines `patch_copy_isolation` and `patch_move_ordering` with frozen source criteria and portable helpers.

- `ghc-family-patch-atomic-policy` combines `patch_atomic_sequences` and `patch_owner_policy` with frozen source criteria and portable helpers.

- `ghc-family-correction-lineage-diff` combines `correction_digest_lineage` and `correction_changed_paths` with frozen source criteria and portable helpers.

- `ghc-family-receipt-scope-manifest` combines `receipt_exact_scope` and `manifest_literal_paths` with frozen source criteria and portable helpers.

- `ghc-family-disclosure-trial-reservations` combines `disclosure_leaf_budget` and `trial_remedy_proxies` with frozen source criteria and portable helpers.

- `ghc-family-measurement-correction-reservations` combines `gmut_measurement_gaps` and `cbr_correction_authority` with frozen source criteria and portable helpers.

Ten packages passed metadata checks and actual positive/adverse CLI use; five unique new shared runners were smoke-used. Global promotion added ten collision-free directories and verified 70 files byte for byte. The catalogue has 15 validated cards and its exact runner query returns five current runners; no trigger overlaps were found. The late Meta Tool Box promotion check remains an operational defect even though the checks subsequently passed. Global availability supplies discoverability, not reload or authority.

Next-owner skill ideas:

- pointer_token_codec review: Decode reference tokens without URI, Unicode, or filesystem reinterpretation.
- pointer_value_resolution review: Resolve a nested JSON value while preserving null, false, zero, and array order.
- pointer_failure_semantics review: Refuse invalid pointer syntax and nonexistent targets before any projection.
- pointer_projection_allowlist review: Project exact leaf or container pointers with explicit missing and overlap behavior.
- patch_add_targets review: Stage JSON add operations on a copy with exact parent and array insertion semantics.
- patch_remove_targets review: Remove only existing synthetic targets; reserve root deletion in this local profile.
- patch_replace_targets review: Replace an existing value without inventing a missing target or coercing its type.
- patch_typed_tests review: Check JSON test predicates with booleans separated from numbers and finite numeric equality.
- patch_copy_isolation review: Copy synthetic values deeply and retain the source on all successful copies.
- patch_move_ordering review: Move after source removal with token-aware descendant refusal and atomic rollback.

Next-owner runner ideas:

- patch_atomic_sequences extension: Add one new falsifiable boundary and preserve the frozen predecessor.
- patch_owner_policy extension: Add one new falsifiable boundary and preserve the frozen predecessor.
- correction_digest_lineage extension: Add one new falsifiable boundary and preserve the frozen predecessor.
- correction_changed_paths extension: Add one new falsifiable boundary and preserve the frozen predecessor.
- receipt_exact_scope extension: Add one new falsifiable boundary and preserve the frozen predecessor.
- manifest_literal_paths extension: Add one new falsifiable boundary and preserve the frozen predecessor.
- disclosure_leaf_budget extension: Add one new falsifiable boundary and preserve the frozen predecessor.
